## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 19 page(s) - 19 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R47: No description](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r47)
**Issue**: The `<meta>` element restricts the ability to zoom

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, maximum-scale=1, user-scalable=no" />
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/meta
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('/meta')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://www.midea.com/de
- https://www.midea.com/de/staubsauger/saugroboter/saugroboter-s8-.s8-
- https://www.midea.com/de/search
- https://www.midea.com/de/staubsauger
- https://www.midea.com/de/error-page
- *...and 14 more page(s)*

## Detailed Failure Information (ALFA)

### https://www.midea.com/de

#### Rule: [SIA-R11: Link elements have an accessible name](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r11)
**WCAG**: [SC 2.4.4](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/link-purpose-in-context), [SC 2.4.9](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/), [SC 4.1.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/name-role-value) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: The link does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<a href="javascript:;" class="gws-notification-bar__inner-btn gws-notification-bar__item-btn" style="cursor: default;" />`
- XPath: `/a[@class="gws-notification-bar__inner-btn gws-notification-bar__item-btn"]`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: The link does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<a href="/de/warenkorb" target="_self">                 </a>`
- XPath: `/a`

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: The link does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<a class="gws-bannerV2__asset-link" href="/de/a-new-journey" target="_self" />`
- XPath: `/a[@class="gws-bannerV2__asset-link"]`

#### Rule: [SIA-R111: Interactive elements have a sufficient target size](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111)
**WCAG**: [SC 2.5.5](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/) (Level AAA)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<button id="onetrust-pc-btn-handler" aria-label="Cookie-Einstellungen, Öffnet das Einstellungscenter-Dialogfeld" aria-haspopup="dialog">Cookie-Einstellungen</button>`
- XPath: `/button[@id="onetrust-pc-btn-handler"]`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<button id="onetrust-reject-all-handler">Alle ablehnen</button>`
- XPath: `/button[@id="onetrust-reject-all-handler"]`

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<button id="onetrust-accept-btn-handler">Alle Cookies akzeptieren</button>`
- XPath: `/button[@id="onetrust-accept-btn-handler"]`

#### Rule: [SIA-R18: ARIA states and properties are allowed for the element on which they are specified](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r18)
**WCAG**: Best Practice

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: The attribute is prohibited for the element on which it is specified

#### Rule: [SIA-R47](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r47)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: The `<meta>` element restricts the ability to zoom
- HTML: `<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, maximum-scale=1, user-scalable=no" />`
- XPath: `/meta`

#### Rule: [SIA-R57: Visible text content on the page is contained within a landmark region](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r57)
**WCAG**: Best Practice

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: The text is not included in a landmark region

#### Rule: [SIA-R66: Text has enhanced contrast with its background](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r66)
**WCAG**: [SC 1.4.6](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/contrast-enhanced) (Level AAA)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: The highest possible contrast of the text is 3.37:1 which is         below the required contrast of 7:1

#### Rule: [SIA-R68: Elements with an explicit ARIA role have the required owned/child elements](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r68)
**WCAG**: [SC 1.3.1](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/info-and-relationships) (Level A)

- XPath: `/ul[@class="gws-top-chat-v1_list"]`

#### Rule: [SIA-R69: Text has sufficient contrast with its background](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r69)
**WCAG**: [SC 1.4.3](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/contrast-minimum), [SC 1.4.6](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/contrast-enhanced) (Level AA)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: The highest possible contrast of the text is 3.37:1 which is         below the required contrast of 4.5:1

**WCAG**: [SC 1.4.8](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/) (Level AAA)

**Failure 1:**

**WCAG**: [SC 2.4.4](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/link-purpose-in-context), [SC 2.4.9](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/), [SC 4.1.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/name-role-value) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: The link does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<a href="javascript:;" class="gws-notification-bar__inner-btn gws-notification-bar__item-btn" style="cursor: default;" />`
- XPath: `/a[@class="gws-notification-bar__inner-btn gws-notification-bar__item-btn"]`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: The link does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<a href="/de/warenkorb" target="_self">                 </a>`
- XPath: `/a`

#### Rule: [SIA-R111: Interactive elements have a sufficient target size](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111)
**WCAG**: [SC 2.5.5](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/) (Level AAA)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a href="https://privacycenter.instagram.com/policy/?entry_point=ig_help_center_data_policy">https://privacycenter.instagram.com/policy/?entry_...</a>`
- XPath: `/a`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a class="facebook" rel="nofollow" href="https://www.facebook.com/MideaDeutschland" target="_blank">...</a>`
- XPath: `/a[@class="facebook"]`

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a class="instagram" rel="nofollow" href="https://www.instagram.com/midea_deutschland/" target="_blank">...</a>`
- XPath: `/a[@class="instagram"]`

**Failure 4:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a class="linkedin" rel="nofollow" href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/mideadeutschland" target="_blank">...</a>`
- XPath: `/a[@class="linkedin"]`

**Failure 5:**
- First identified: 2026-11-03
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a class="youtube" rel="nofollow" href="https://www.youtube.com/@mideadeutschland" target="_blank">...</a>`
- XPath: `/a[@class="youtube"]`

*... and 5 more failures for this rule*

#### Rule: [SIA-R66: Text has enhanced contrast with its background](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r66)
**WCAG**: [SC 1.4.6](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/contrast-enhanced) (Level AAA)

#### Rule: [SIA-R68: Elements with an explicit ARIA role have the required owned/child elements](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r68)

**WCAG**: [SC 1.4.3](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/contrast-minimum), [SC 1.4.6](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/contrast-enhanced) (Level AA)

**Failure 1:**

#### Rule: [SIA-R75](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r75)

- HTML: `<div class="car-numb">0</div>`
- XPath: `/div[@class="car-numb"]`

- HTML: `<input type="checkbox" id="gdpr_21105" name="gdpr[21105]" class="gdpr" value="Y" />`
- XPath: `/input[@id="gdpr_21105"]`
